Bible Quiz Questions – Old Testament – Volume 3: Historical Books II
1 Chronicles–Esther · 98 chapters · 784 questions
Collection index and Volume 1
Each chapter has eight questions: three Easy, three Medium and two Hard. The question order and multiple-choice answer positions are mixed. Difficulty is editorial: Hard questions require closer recall, comparison of passages or careful interpretation.
Quotation questions marked WEB use the World English Bible. Other questions preserve the study material’s wording or paraphrase a passage. Accept a clearly equivalent answer when exact wording is not being tested.
Read the passage in context before applying it today or connecting it to Christ. A character’s statement is not automatically the narrator’s conclusion. Theological questions use the course summaries listed below alongside Scripture; disputed readings are identified in the explanations.
Sources: Old testament course summary · Gospel Worldview course summary · World English Bible
1 Chronicles
1 Chronicles 1
Q1. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… in his days the earth was divided; and his brother's name was ______.”? (1:19, WEB) Medium
A) Eber B) Ithran C) Noah D) Joktan
Answer: D. Joktan. The passage specifies Joktan: “… in his days the earth was divided; and his brother's name was Joktan.”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “Now these are the kings who reigned in the land of ______, before there resigned any king over the children of Israel: Bela the son of …” (1:43); “… Hadad the son of Bedad, who struck Midian in the field of ______, reigned in his place; and the name of his city was Avith.” (1:46, WEB) Hard
Answer: Edom; Moab. The first passage reads “Now these are the kings who reigned in the land of Edom, before there resigned any king over the children of Israel: Bela the son of …”; the second reads “… Hadad the son of Bedad, who struck Midian in the field of Moab, reigned in his place; and the name of his city was Avith.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “The sons of Cush: ______, and Havilah, and Sabta, and Raama, and Sabteca. The sons of Raamah: Sheba, and Dedan.”? (1:9, WEB) Easy
A) Seba B) Ephah C) struck D) Medan
Answer: A. Seba. 1 Chronicles 1:9 uses Seba: “The sons of Cush: Seba, and Havilah, and Sabta, and Raama, and Sabteca. The sons of Raamah: Sheba, and Dedan.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “The sons of ______: Elam, and Asshur, and Arpachshad, and Lud, and Aram, and Uz, and Hul, and ______, and Meshech.” (1:17, WEB) Medium
Answer: Shem; Gether. The wording is “The sons of Shem: Elam, and Asshur, and Arpachshad, and Lud, and Aram, and Uz, and Hul, and Gether, and Meshech.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “The sons of Keturah, Abraham's concubine: she bore ______, and Jokshan, and Medan, and Midian, and Ishbak, and Shuah. The sons of Jokshan: Sheba, and Dedan.” (1:32); “The sons of ______: Teman, and Omar, Zephi, and Gatam, Kenaz, and Timna, and Amalek.” (1:36, WEB) Hard
A) Zimran; Eliphaz B) Zimran; Judah C) Anamim; Eliphaz D) Eliphaz; Zimran
Answer: A. Zimran; Eliphaz. Verse 32 reads “The sons of Keturah, Abraham's concubine: she bore Zimran, and Jokshan, and Medan, and Midian, and Ishbak, and Shuah. The sons of Jokshan: Sheba, and Dedan.”; verse 36 reads “The sons of Eliphaz: Teman, and Omar, Zephi, and Gatam, Kenaz, and Timna, and Amalek.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “The sons of ______: Gomer, and Magog, and Madai, and Javan, and Tubal, and Meshech, and Tiras.” (1:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: Japheth. The cited words are “The sons of Japheth: Gomer, and Magog, and Madai, and Javan, and Tubal, and Meshech, and Tiras.”
Q7. True or false: The genealogy begins with Adam. (1:1) Easy
Answer: True. 1 Chronicles 1:1 records: “Adam, Seth, Enosh,”
Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Serug, reigned, Terah,” (1:26, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Serug, Nahor, Terah,” It says Nahor, not reigned.
1 Chronicles 2
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Ozem the ______, David the ______;” (2:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: sixth; seventh. The wording is “Ozem the sixth, David the seventh;” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… and Amminadab became the father of Nahshon, prince of the children of ______;”? (2:10, WEB) Easy
A) Samuel B) Moses C) Judah D) Josiah
Answer: C. Judah. 1 Chronicles 2:10 uses Judah: “… and Amminadab became the father of Nahshon, prince of the children of Judah;”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… the father of Ziph; and the sons of Mareshah the father of ______.” (2:42); “… of Jahdai: Regem, and Jothan, and Geshan, and Pelet, and Ephah, and ______.” (2:47, WEB) Hard
Answer: Hebron; Shaaph. The first passage reads “… the father of Ziph; and the sons of Mareshah the father of Hebron.”; the second reads “… of Jahdai: Regem, and Jothan, and Geshan, and Pelet, and Ephah, and Shaaph.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Afterward Hezron went in to the daughter of Machir the father of ______, whom he took as wife when he was sixty years old; and she …”? (2:21, WEB) Medium
A) Sinai B) Edom C) Gilead D) Babylon
Answer: C. Gilead. The passage specifies Gilead: “Afterward Hezron went in to the daughter of Machir the father of Gilead, whom he took as wife when he was sixty years old; and she …”
Q5. True or false: The opening list names Judah among the sons of Israel, also called Jacob. (2:1) Easy
Answer: True. The verse lists the sons beginning with Reuben, Simeon, Levi and Judah. Genesis 29:35 supplies Judah's birth account.
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… of Hezron were Ram the firstborn, and Bunah, and Oren, and Ozem, Haroeh.” (2:25, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… of Hezron were Ram the firstborn, and Bunah, and Oren, and Ozem, Ahijah.” It says Ahijah, not Haroeh.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… daughter-in-law bore him Perez and Zerah. All the sons of Judah were ______.” (2:4, WEB) Easy
Answer: five. The cited words are “… daughter-in-law bore him Perez and Zerah. All the sons of Judah were five.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “The sons of Appaim: Ishi. The sons of Ishi: ______. The sons of Sheshan: Ahlai.” (2:31); “Attai became the father of Nathan, and ______ became the father of Zabad,” (2:36, WEB) Hard
A) Nathan; Sheshan B) Sheshan; Nathan C) Sheshan; Caleb D) Jether; Nathan
Answer: B. Sheshan; Nathan. Verse 31 reads “The sons of Appaim: Ishi. The sons of Ishi: Sheshan. The sons of Sheshan: Ahlai.”; verse 36 reads “Attai became the father of Nathan, and Nathan became the father of Zabad,”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
1 Chronicles 3
Q1. True or false: David's sons were born in Hebron and Jerusalem. (3:1-4) Easy
Answer: True. 1 Chronicles 3:1 records: “Now these were the sons of David, who were born to him in Hebron: the firstborn, Amnon, of Ahinoam the Jezreelitess; the second, Daniel, of Abigail the Carmelitess;”
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… were born to him in Jerusalem: Shimea, and Shobab, and Nathan, and ______, four, of Bathshua the daughter of Ammiel;” (3:5, WEB) Easy
Answer: Solomon. The cited words are “… were born to him in Jerusalem: Shimea, and Shobab, and Nathan, and Solomon, four, of Bathshua the daughter of Ammiel;”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Solomon's son was Rehoboam, ______ his son, Asa his son, Jehoshaphat his son,”? (3:10, WEB) Medium
A) Ahinoam B) Abijah C) Jerusalem D) Shemaiah
Answer: B. Abijah. The passage specifies Abijah: “Solomon's son was Rehoboam, Abijah his son, Asa his son, Jehoshaphat his son,”
Q4. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Amaziah his son, Azariah his son, Jotham his son,” (3:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “Amaziah his son, Azariah his son, Jotham his son,”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “and Hashubah, and Ohel, and Berechiah, and Hasadiah, Jushab-hesed, ______.” (3:20); “… Jeshaiah; the sons of Rephaiah, the sons of Arnan, the sons of ______, the sons of Shecaniah.” (3:21, WEB) Hard
Answer: five; Obadiah. The first passage reads “and Hashubah, and Ohel, and Berechiah, and Hasadiah, Jushab-hesed, five.”; the second reads “… Jeshaiah; the sons of Rephaiah, the sons of Arnan, the sons of Obadiah, the sons of Shecaniah.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “and Ibhar, and Elishama, and ______,”? (3:6, WEB) Easy
A) Ohel B) Eliphelet C) daughter D) Shallum
Answer: B. Eliphelet. 1 Chronicles 3:6 uses Eliphelet: “and Ibhar, and Elishama, and Eliphelet,”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “and ______, and Eliada, and Eliphelet, ______.” (3:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: Elishama; nine. The wording is “and Elishama, and Eliada, and Eliphelet, nine.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “The sons of ______: the firstborn Johanan, the second Jehoiakim, the third Zedekiah, the fourth Shallum.” (3:15); “The sons of Pedaiah: ______, and Shimei. The sons of Zerubbabel: Meshullam, and Hananiah; and Shelomith was their sister;” (3:19, WEB) Hard
A) Esau; Zerubbabel B) Josiah; Zerubbabel C) Zerubbabel; Josiah D) Josiah; Jesse
Answer: B. Josiah; Zerubbabel. Verse 15 reads “The sons of Josiah: the firstborn Johanan, the second Jehoiakim, the third Zedekiah, the fourth Shallum.”; verse 19 reads “The sons of Pedaiah: Zerubbabel, and Shimei. The sons of Zerubbabel: Meshullam, and Hananiah; and Shelomith was their sister;”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
1 Chronicles 4
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… father of Beth Rapha, and Paseah, and ______ the father of Ir Nahash. These are the men of ______.” (4:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: Tehinnah; Recah. The wording is “… father of Beth Rapha, and Paseah, and Tehinnah the father of Ir Nahash. These are the men of Recah.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “The sons of Shimon: Amnon, and Rinnah, Ben Hanan, and Tilon. The sons of Ishi: Zoheth, and Ben Zoheth.” (4:20, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “The sons of Shimon: Amnon, and Rinnah, Ben Hanan, and Tilon. The sons of Ishi: Zoheth, and Ben Zoheth.”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… of Ezrah: Jether, and Mered, and Epher, and Jalon; and she bore ______, and Shammai, and Ishbah the father of Eshtemoa.”? (4:17, WEB) Medium
A) Miriam B) Manasseh C) Rachel D) Abraham
Answer: A. Miriam. The passage specifies Miriam: “… of Ezrah: Jether, and Mered, and Epher, and Jalon; and she bore Miriam, and Shammai, and Ishbah the father of Eshtemoa.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “The sons of ______: Nemuel, and Jamin, Jarib, Zerah, Shaul;” (4:24); “Shimei had ______ sons and six daughters; but his brothers didn't have many children, neither did all their family multiply like the children of Judah.” (4:27, WEB) Hard
A) Reuben; sixteen B) Simeon; sixteen C) sixteen; Simeon D) Simeon; fifteen
Answer: B. Simeon; sixteen. Verse 24 reads “The sons of Simeon: Nemuel, and Jamin, Jarib, Zerah, Shaul;”; verse 27 reads “Shimei had sixteen sons and six daughters; but his brothers didn't have many children, neither did all their family multiply like the children of Judah.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Hakkoz became the father of ______, and Zobebah, and the families of Aharhel the son of Harum.”? (4:8, WEB) Easy
A) name B) flocks C) Anub D) Shelah
Answer: C. Anub. 1 Chronicles 4:8 uses Anub: “Hakkoz became the father of Anub, and Zobebah, and the families of Aharhel the son of Harum.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “and all their villages that were round about the same cities, to ______. These were their habitations, and they have their genealogy.” (4:33); “these mentioned by name were ______ in their families: and their fathers' houses increased greatly.” (4:38, WEB) Hard
Answer: Baal; princes. The first passage reads “and all their villages that were round about the same cities, to Baal. These were their habitations, and they have their genealogy.”; the second reads “these mentioned by name were princes in their families: and their fathers' houses increased greatly.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “These were the sons of the father of Etam: ______, and Ishma, and Idbash; and the name of their sister was Hazzelelponi;” (4:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jezreel. The cited words are “These were the sons of the father of Etam: Jezreel, and Ishma, and Idbash; and the name of their sister was Hazzelelponi;”
Q8. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, jabez prayed that God would enlarge his ______. (4:10) Easy
Answer: territory. 1 Chronicles 4:10 records: “… Israel, saying, Oh that you would bless me indeed, and enlarge my border, and that your hand might be with me, and that you would keep me from evil, that it not be to my sorrow! …”
1 Chronicles 5
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “the sons of ______ the firstborn of Israel: Hanoch, and Pallu, Hezron, and Carmi.” (5:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Reuben. The cited words are “the sons of Reuben the firstborn of Israel: Hanoch, and Pallu, Hezron, and Carmi.”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “______ the son of Abdiel, the son of Guni, chief of their fathers' houses.” (5:15); “… and sword, and to shoot with bow, and skillful in war, were forty-______ thousand seven hundred and sixty, that were able to go forth to war.” (5:18, WEB) Hard
A) Ahi; four B) firstborn; four C) four; Ahi D) Ahi; twelve
Answer: A. Ahi; four. Verse 15 reads “Ahi the son of Abdiel, the son of Guni, chief of their fathers' houses.”; verse 18 reads “… and sword, and to shoot with bow, and skillful in war, were forty-four thousand seven hundred and sixty, that were able to go forth to war.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… and Meshullam, and Sheba, and Jorai, and Jacan, and Zia, and Eber, seven.” (5:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… and Meshullam, and Sheba, and Jorai, and Jacan, and Zia, and Eber, seven.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… and they lived in their tents throughout all the land east of ______.”? (5:10, WEB) Medium
A) Gilead B) Nineveh C) Jordan D) Jerusalem
Answer: A. Gilead. The passage specifies Gilead: “… and they lived in their tents throughout all the land east of Gilead.”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which word completes “______ his son, Reaiah his son, Baal his son,”? (5:5, WEB) Easy
A) Micah B) Sarah C) Ham D) Obadiah
Answer: A. Micah. 1 Chronicles 5:5 uses Micah: “Micah his son, Reaiah his son, Baal his son,”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… the son of Shema, the son of ______, who lived in Aroer, even to Nebo and Baal ______:” (5:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: Joel; Meon. The wording is “… the son of Shema, the son of Joel, who lived in Aroer, even to Nebo and Baal Meon:” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. True or false: The Reubenites fought and were helped by God because they trusted Him. (5:20) Easy
Answer: True. 1 Chronicles 5:20 records: “… helped against them, and the Hagrites were delivered into their hand, and all who were with them; for they cried to God in the battle, and he was entreated of them, because they put their trust …”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… their cattle; of their camels fifty thousand, and of sheep two hundred ______ thousand, and of donkeys two thousand, and of men one hundred thousand.” (5:21); “The children of the half-tribe of ______ lived in the land: they increased from Bashan to Baal Hermon and Senir and Mount Hermon.” (5:23, WEB) Hard
Answer: fifty; Manasseh. The first passage reads “… their cattle; of their camels fifty thousand, and of sheep two hundred fifty thousand, and of donkeys two thousand, and of men one hundred thousand.”; the second reads “The children of the half-tribe of Manasseh lived in the land: they increased from Bashan to Baal Hermon and Senir and Mount Hermon.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
1 Chronicles 6
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “The sons of ______: Amminadab his son, ______ his son, Assir his son,” (6:22, WEB) Medium
Answer: Kohath; Korah. The wording is “The sons of Kohath: Amminadab his son, Korah his son, Assir his son,” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… out of the tribe of Issachar, and out of the tribe of ______, and out of the tribe of Naphtali, and out of the tribe of …” (6:62); “and out of the half-tribe of ______, Aner with its suburbs, and Bileam with its suburbs, for the rest of the family of the sons of …” (6:70, WEB) Hard
Answer: Asher; Manasseh. The first passage reads “… out of the tribe of Issachar, and out of the tribe of Asher, and out of the tribe of Naphtali, and out of the tribe of …”; the second reads “and out of the half-tribe of Manasseh, Aner with its suburbs, and Bileam with its suburbs, for the rest of the family of the sons of …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “The children of Amram: ______, and Moses, and Miriam. The sons of Aaron: Nadab, and Abihu, Eleazar, and Ithamar.” (6:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Aaron. The cited words are “The children of Amram: Aaron, and Moses, and Miriam. The sons of Aaron: Nadab, and Abihu, Eleazar, and Ithamar.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… Ethan the son of Kishi, the son of Abdi, the son of ______,” (6:44); “… of Aaron, of the families of the Kohathites (for theirs was the ______ lot),” (6:54, WEB) Hard
A) Malluch; sixty B) Malluch; first C) first; Malluch D) Zebulun; first
Answer: B. Malluch; first. Verse 44 reads “… Ethan the son of Kishi, the son of Abdi, the son of Malluch,”; verse 54 reads “… of Aaron, of the families of the Kohathites (for theirs was the first lot),”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, heman was a singer in the house of the ______. (6:31-33) Easy
Answer: LORD. 1 Chronicles 6:31 records: “These are they whom David set over the service of song in the house of Yahweh, after that the ark had rest.”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “His brother Asaph, who stood on his right hand, even Asaph the son of Berechiah, the son of Shimea,” (6:39, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “His brother Asaph, who stood on his right hand, even Asaph the son of Berechiah, the son of Shimea,”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “and Shallum became the father of ______, and Hilkiah became the father of Azariah,”? (6:13, WEB) Easy
A) Ithamar B) Hilkiah C) Hashabiah D) Ethan
Answer: B. Hilkiah. 1 Chronicles 6:13 uses Hilkiah: “and Shallum became the father of Hilkiah, and Hilkiah became the father of Azariah,”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “The sons of Merari: Mahli, Libni his son, Shimei his son, ______ his son,”? (6:29, WEB) Medium
A) Jokmeam B) Ephraim C) Galilee D) Uzzah
Answer: D. Uzzah. The passage specifies Uzzah: “The sons of Merari: Mahli, Libni his son, Shimei his son, Uzzah his son,”
1 Chronicles 7
Q1. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… mighty men of valor; and they were reckoned by genealogy twenty-two thousand ______-four.”? (7:7, WEB) Easy
A) fifteen B) thirty C) ten D) seventh
Answer: B. thirty. 1 Chronicles 7:7 uses thirty: “… mighty men of valor; and they were reckoned by genealogy twenty-two thousand thirty-four.”
Q2. True or false: The tribe of Benjamin's families are listed by name. (7:6) Easy
Answer: True. 1 Chronicles 7:6 records: “The sons of Benjamin: Bela, and Becher, and Jediael, three.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… according to the heads of their fathers' houses, mighty men of valor, ______ ______ and two hundred, who were able to go forth in the host …” (7:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: seventeen; thousand. The wording is “… according to the heads of their fathers' houses, mighty men of valor, seventeen thousand and two hundred, who were able to go forth in the host …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… and Shuppim, whose sister's name was Maacah; and the name of the ______ was Zelophehad: and Zelophehad had daughters.”? (7:15, WEB) Medium
A) seventh B) seventeen C) second D) seventy
Answer: C. second. The passage specifies second: “… and Shuppim, whose sister's name was Maacah; and the name of the second was Zelophehad: and Zelophehad had daughters.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… Uzzi: Izrahiah. The sons of Izrahiah: Michael, and Obadiah, and Joel, Isshiah, ______; all of them chief men.” (7:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: five. The cited words are “… Uzzi: Izrahiah. The sons of Izrahiah: Michael, and Obadiah, and Joel, Isshiah, five; all of them chief men.”
Q6. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “The sons of Ephraim: Shuthelah, and Bered his son, and Tahath his son, and Eleadah his son, and Tahath his son,” (7:20, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “The sons of Ephraim: Shuthelah, and Bered his son, and Tahath his son, and Eleadah his son, and Tahath his son,”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “The sons of ______: Imnah, and Ishvah, and Ishvi, and Beriah, and Serah their sister.” (7:30); “The sons of Helem his brother: Zophah, and ______, and Shelesh, and Amal.” (7:35, WEB) Hard
Answer: Asher; Imna. The first passage reads “The sons of Asher: Imnah, and Ishvah, and Ishvi, and Beriah, and Serah their sister.”; the second reads “The sons of Helem his brother: Zophah, and Imna, and Shelesh, and Amal.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q8. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “He went in to his wife, and she conceived, and ______ a son, and he named him Beriah, because it went evil with his house.” (7:23); “Their possessions and habitations were ______ and the towns of it, and eastward Naaran, and westward Gezer, with the towns of it; Shechem also and the …” (7:28, WEB) Hard
A) bore; Bethel B) lower; Bethel C) Bethel; bore D) bore; Samaria
Answer: A. bore; Bethel. Verse 23 reads “He went in to his wife, and she conceived, and bore a son, and he named him Beriah, because it went evil with his house.”; verse 28 reads “Their possessions and habitations were Bethel and the towns of it, and eastward Naaran, and westward Gezer, with the towns of it; Shechem also and the …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
1 Chronicles 8
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “and Ishmerai, and Izliah, and Jobab, the sons of Elpaal,” (8:18, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “and Ishmerai, and Izliah, and Jobab, the sons of Elpaal,”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “and Abdon, and ______, and Hanan,” (8:23); “and Jaareshiah, and ______, and Zichri, the sons of Jeroham.” (8:27, WEB) Hard
A) Zichri; Elijah B) Elijah; Zichri C) Eleasah; Elijah D) Zichri; Habakkuk
Answer: A. Zichri; Elijah. Verse 23 reads “and Abdon, and Zichri, and Hanan,”; verse 27 reads “and Jaareshiah, and Elijah, and Zichri, the sons of Jeroham.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “______ had sons: Addar, and Gera, and Abihud,” (8:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Bela. The cited words are “Bela had sons: Addar, and Gera, and Abihud,”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “and Naaman, and Ahijah, and ______, he carried them captive: and he became the father of Uzza and Ahihud.”? (8:7, WEB) Easy
A) Ahihud B) Elpaal C) Saul D) Gera
Answer: D. Gera. 1 Chronicles 8:7 uses Gera: “and Naaman, and Ahijah, and Gera, he carried them captive: and he became the father of Uzza and Ahihud.”
Q5. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, jonathan's son Merib-baal was the grandson of ______. (8:33-34) Easy
Answer: Saul. 1 Chronicles 8:33 records: “Ner became the father of Kish; and Kish became the father of Saul; and Saul became the father of Jonathan, and Malchishua, and Abinadab, and Eshbaal.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “The sons of ______: Eber, and Misham, and Shemed, who built Ono and Lod, with the ______ of it;” (8:12, WEB) Medium
Answer: Elpaal; towns. The wording is “The sons of Elpaal: Eber, and Misham, and Shemed, who built Ono and Lod, with the towns of it;” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q7. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “and his firstborn son ______, and Zur, and Kish, and Baal, and Nadab,” (8:30); “The sons of ______: Pithon, and Melech, and Tarea, and Ahaz.” (8:35, WEB) Hard
Answer: Abdon; Micah. The first passage reads “and his firstborn son Abdon, and Zur, and Kish, and Baal, and Nadab,”; the second reads “The sons of Micah: Pithon, and Melech, and Tarea, and Ahaz.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

1 Chronicles 9
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Nebuchadnezzar the son of Meshelemiah was porter of the door of the tent of meeting.” (9:21, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Zechariah the son of Meshelemiah was porter of the door of the tent of meeting.” It says Zechariah, not Nebuchadnezzar.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… Jerusalem lived of the children of Judah, and of the children of ______, and of the children of Ephraim and Manasseh:” (9:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: Benjamin. The cited words are “… Jerusalem lived of the children of Judah, and of the children of Benjamin, and of the children of Ephraim and Manasseh:”
Q3. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “for the ______ chief porters, who were Levites, were in an office of trust, and were over the chambers and over the treasuries in the house …” (9:26); “… the sanctuary, and over the fine flour, and the wine, and the ______, and the frankincense, and the spices.” (9:29, WEB) Hard

Answer: C. four; oil. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “and ______ the son of Jeroham, and Elah the son of Uzzi, the son of Michri, and Meshullam the son of Shephatiah, the son of Reuel, …”? (9:8, WEB) Easy

Q5. True or false: Gatekeepers were chosen to guard the house of the LORD. (9:22) Easy
Answer: True. 1 Chronicles 9:22 records: “All these who were chosen to be porters in the thresholds were two hundred and twelve. These were reckoned by genealogy in their villages, whom David and Samuel the seer did ordain in their office of …”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “and Adaiah the son of Jeroham, the son of ______, the son of Malchijah, and ______ the son of Adiel, the son of Jahzerah, the son …” (9:12, WEB) Medium

Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “and ______ the son of Shemaiah, the son of Galal, the son of Jeduthun, and Berechiah the son of Asa, the son of Elkanah, who lived …”? (9:16, WEB) Medium

Q8. 1 Chronicles 10
Q1. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… him, and took his head, and his armor, and sent into the ______ of the Philistines round about, to carry the news to their idols, and …” (10:9); “They put his ______ in the house of their gods, and fastened his head in the house of Dagon.” (10:10, WEB) Hard
A) land; armor B) heaven; armor C) land; reason D) armor; land
Answer: A. land; armor. Verse 9 reads “… him, and took his head, and his armor, and sent into the land of the Philistines round about, to carry the news to their idols, and …”; verse 10 reads “They put his armor in the house of their gods, and fastened his head in the house of Dagon.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “It happened on the next day, when the Philistines came to news the slain, that they found Saul and his sons fallen on Mount Gilboa.” (10:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “It happened on the next day, when the Philistines came to strip the slain, that they found Saul and his sons fallen on Mount Gilboa.” It says strip, not news.
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… followed hard after Saul and after his sons; and the Philistines killed ______, and Abinadab, and Malchishua, the sons of Saul.” (10:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Jonathan. The cited words are “… followed hard after Saul and after his sons; and the Philistines killed Jonathan, and Abinadab, and Malchishua, the sons of Saul.”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “When all the men of ______ who were in the valley saw that they fled, and that Saul and his sons were dead, they forsook their …”? (10:7, WEB) Medium

Answer: C. Israel. The passage specifies Israel: “When all the men of Israel who were in the valley saw that they fled, and that Saul and his sons were dead, they forsook their …”

Answer: C. Saul. 1 Chronicles 10:3 uses Saul: “The battle went sore against Saul, and the archers overtook him; and he was distressed by reason of the archers.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… Jabesh, and buried their bones under the oak in Jabesh, and fasted ______ days.” (10:12); “… he killed him, and turned the kingdom to David the son of ______.” (10:14, WEB) Hard
Answer: seven; Jesse. The first passage reads “… Jabesh, and buried their bones under the oak in Jabesh, and fasted seven days.”; the second reads “… he killed him, and turned the kingdom to David the son of Jesse.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. True or false: The narrator links Saul's death with unfaithfulness, disobedience and consulting a medium instead of seeking the LORD. (10:13-14) Easy
Answer: True. The closing explanation lists these failures together. Consulting the medium is not the only reason the narrator names.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Then said Saul to his armor-bearer, ______ your sword, and thrust me through therewith, lest these uncircumcised come and ______ me. But his armor-bearer would not; …” (10:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: Draw; abuse. The wording is “Then said Saul to his armor-bearer, Draw your sword, and thrust me through therewith, lest these uncircumcised come and abuse me. But his armor-bearer would not; …” The missing terms belong in that order.

Answer: False. The actual wording is “but I will settle him in my house and in my kingdom forever; and his throne shall be established forever.” It says forever, not wherever.

1 Chronicles 21

Answer: False. The actual wording is “God sent an angel to Jerusalem to destroy it: and as he was about to destroy, Yahweh saw, and he repented him of the evil, and …” It says Jerusalem, not Israel.

Answer: Gad. The cited words are “Yahweh spoke to Gad, David's seer, saying,”
Q3. True or false: David sinned by counting the people of Israel. (21:1-8) Easy
Answer: True. 1 Chronicles 21:1 records: “Satan stood up against Israel, and moved David to number Israel.”
Q4. Multiple choice: How should this account be compared with 2 Samuel 24:1? (21:1-8) Hard
A) Chronicles denies David made any decision. B) Samuel says no census occurred. C) Both accounts use exactly the same wording about the inciter. D) Chronicles names Satan as the inciter, while Samuel describes the event in relation to God's anger.
Answer: D. Chronicles names Satan as the inciter, while Samuel describes the event in relation to God's anger.. The accounts use different perspectives on agency. Readers should acknowledge the difference rather than pretend the wording is identical; both narratives hold David responsible for his action.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… Gad to tell David, that David should go up, and raise an ______ to Yahweh in the threshing floor of Ornan the Jebusite.” (21:18); “As David came to Ornan, ______ looked and saw David, and went out of the threshing floor, and bowed himself to David with his face to …” (21:21, WEB) Hard

These establish the first and second answers respectively.

A) servants; first B) first; servants C) father; first D) servants; forty
Answer: A. servants; first. Verse 11 reads “Thus they returned us answer, saying, We are the servants of the God of heaven and earth, and are building the house that was built these …”; verse 13 reads “But in the first year of Cyrus king of Babylon, Cyrus the king made a decree to build this house of God.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Ezra 6
Q1. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, king Darius found the decree of ______ and confirmed the building. (6:3) Easy
Answer: Cyrus. Ezra 6:3 records: “… the king made a decree: Concerning the house of God at Jerusalem, let the house be built, the place where they offer sacrifices, and let the foundations of it be strongly laid; the height of it …”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which word completes “with ______ courses of great stones, and a course of new timber: and let the expenses be given out of the king's house.”? (6:4, WEB) Easy
A) three B) thirteen C) twenty D) fourth
Answer: A. three. Ezra 6:4 uses three: “with three courses of great stones, and a course of new timber: and let the expenses be given out of the king's house.”
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… pulled out from his house, and let him be lifted up and fastened thereon; and let his house be made a dunghill for this:” (6:11, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… pulled out from his house, and let him be lifted up and fastened thereon; and let his house be made a dunghill for this:”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “Now therefore, Tattenai, ______ ______ the River, Shetharbozenai, and your companions the Apharsachites, who are beyond the River, be you far from there:” (6:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: governor; beyond. The wording is “Now therefore, Tattenai, governor beyond the River, Shetharbozenai, and your companions the Apharsachites, who are beyond the River, be you far from there:” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “They set the ______ in their divisions, and the Levites in their courses, for the service of God, which is at Jerusalem; as it is written …” (6:18); “… pure: and they killed the Passover for all the children of the ______, and for their brothers the priests, and for themselves.” (6:20, WEB) Hard
Answer: priests; captivity. The first passage reads “They set the priests in their divisions, and the Levites in their courses, for the service of God, which is at Jerusalem; as it is written …”; the second reads “… pure: and they killed the Passover for all the children of the captivity, and for their brothers the priests, and for themselves.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… found at Achmetha, in the palace that is in the province of ______, a scroll, and therein was thus written for a record:” (6:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Media. The cited words are “… found at Achmetha, in the palace that is in the province of Media, a scroll, and therein was thus written for a record:”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… lambs, for burnt offerings to the God of heaven; also wheat, salt, ______, and oil, according to the word of the priests who are at Jerusalem, …”? (6:9, WEB) Medium
A) barley B) wheat C) salt D) wine
Answer: D. wine. The passage specifies wine: “… lambs, for burnt offerings to the God of heaven; also wheat, salt, wine, and oil, according to the word of the priests who are at Jerusalem, …”
Q8. Ezra 7
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “Now after these things, in the reign of Artaxerxes king of Persia, ______ the son of Seraiah, the son of Azariah, the son of Hilkiah,” (7:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Ezra. The cited words are “Now after these things, in the reign of Artaxerxes king of Persia, Ezra the son of Seraiah, the son of Azariah, the son of Hilkiah,”
Q2. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… a decree, that all those of the people of Israel, and their fathers and the Levites, in my realm, who are minded of their own free …” (7:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… a decree, that all those of the people of Israel, and their priests and the Levites, in my realm, who are minded of their own free …” It says priests, not fathers.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which word completes “the son of Abishua, the son of ______, the son of Eleazar, the son of Aaron the chief priest;”? (7:5, WEB) Easy
A) Nahum B) Naphtali C) Enoch D) Phinehas
Answer: D. Phinehas. Ezra 7:5 uses Phinehas: “the son of Abishua, the son of Phinehas, the son of Eleazar, the son of Aaron the chief priest;”
Q4. True or false: Ezra was a scribe skilled in the Law of Moses. (7:6) Easy
Answer: True. Ezra 7:6 records: “… up from Babylon: and he was a ready scribe in the law of Moses, which Yahweh, the God of Israel, had given; and the king granted him all his request, according to the hand of Yahweh …”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “and all the ______ and gold that you shall find in all the province of Babylon, with the freewill-offering of the people, and of the priests, …” (7:16); “… which you shall have occasion to bestow, bestow it out of the ______ treasure-house.” (7:20, WEB) Hard
A) copper; king's B) silver; king's C) king's; silver D) silver; servants
Answer: B. silver; king's. Verse 16 reads “and all the silver and gold that you shall find in all the province of Babylon, with the freewill-offering of the people, and of the priests, …”; verse 20 reads “… which you shall have occasion to bestow, bestow it out of the king's treasure-house.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… scribe of the words of the commandments of Yahweh, and of his ______ to Israel:”? (7:11, WEB) Medium
A) commandments B) statutes C) law D) promise
Answer: B. statutes. The passage specifies statutes: “… scribe of the words of the commandments of Yahweh, and of his statutes to Israel:”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… first month began he to go up from ______; and on the first day of the ______ month came he to Jerusalem, according to the good …” (7:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: Babylon; fifth. The wording is “… first month began he to go up from Babylon; and on the first day of the fifth month came he to Jerusalem, according to the good …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… to one hundred measures of wheat, and to one hundred baths of ______, and to one hundred baths of oil, and salt without prescribing how much.” (7:22); “You, Ezra, after the ______ of your God who is in your hand, appoint magistrates and judges, who may judge all the people who are beyond …” (7:25, WEB) Hard
Answer: wine; wisdom. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Q1. Answer: False. The actual wording is “I sent them forth to Iddo the chief at the place Casiphia; and I told them what they should tell Iddo, and his brothers the Nethinim, …” It says Casiphia, not straight.

Ezra 10
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Of the sons of Harim: Maaseiah, and Darius, and Shemaiah, and Jehiel, and Uzziah.” (10:21, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Of the sons of Harim: Maaseiah, and Elijah, and Shemaiah, and Jehiel, and Uzziah.” It says Elijah, not Darius.
Q2. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the people agreed to separate from their foreign ______. (10:11-12) Easy
Answer: wives. Ezra 10:11 records: “Now therefore make confession to Yahweh, the God of your fathers, and do his pleasure; and separate yourselves from the peoples of the land, and from the foreign women.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… commandment of our God; and let it be done according to the ______.” (10:3, WEB) Easy
Answer: law. The cited words are “… commandment of our God; and let it be done according to the law.”
Q4. Multiple choice: What context is necessary when reading the community's separation from foreign wives? (10:1-17; 9:1-2) Hard
A) Every interethnic marriage is forbidden in every setting. B) The book denies that foreigners can worship God. C) The narrative concerns covenant unfaithfulness in the returned community and presents a difficult historical response. D) No covenant concerns appear in Ezra.
Answer: C. The narrative concerns covenant unfaithfulness in the returned community and presents a difficult historical response.. It must be read beside the larger biblical witness, including Ruth and God's welcome of foreigners. It is not a general claim that mixed ethnicity makes a marriage sinful.
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… the sons of Parosh: Ramiah, and Izziah, and Malchijah, and Mijamin, and ______, and Malchijah, and Benaiah.” (10:25); “… of Pahath-moab: Adna, and Chelal, Benaiah, Maaseiah, Mattaniah, Bezalel, and Binnui, and ______.” (10:30, WEB) Hard
A) Eleazar; Nahum B) Leah; Manasseh C) Eleazar; Manasseh D) Manasseh; Eleazar
Answer: C. Eleazar; Manasseh. Verse 25 reads “… the sons of Parosh: Ramiah, and Izziah, and Malchijah, and Mijamin, and Eleazar, and Malchijah, and Benaiah.”; verse 30 reads “… of Pahath-moab: Adna, and Chelal, Benaiah, Maaseiah, Mattaniah, Bezalel, and Binnui, and Manasseh.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “and that whoever didn't come within ______ days, according to the counsel of the princes and the elders, all his substance should be forfeited, and himself …”? (10:8, WEB) Easy
A) seven B) forty C) thousand D) three
Answer: D. three. Ezra 10:8 uses three: “and that whoever didn't come within three days, according to the counsel of the princes and the elders, all his substance should be forfeited, and himself …”
Q7. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “The children of the captivity did so. ______ the priest, with certain heads of fathers' houses, after their fathers' houses, and all of them by their …”? (10:16, WEB) Medium
A) Ezra B) Hezekiah C) Enoch D) Obed
Answer: A. Ezra. The passage specifies Ezra: “The children of the captivity did so. Ezra the priest, with certain heads of fathers' houses, after their fathers' houses, and all of them by their …”
Q8. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… is this a work of one day or ______; for we have greatly transgressed in this ______.” (10:13, WEB) Medium
Answer: two; matter. The wording is “… is this a work of one day or two; for we have greatly transgressed in this matter.” The missing terms belong in that order.

Answer: gate. The cited words are “The fish gate did the sons of Hassenaah build; they laid the beams of it, and set up the doors of it, the bolts of it, …”
Q5. Answer: stands; priests. Q1. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, nehemiah armed the workers with swords, spears and ______. (4:13) Easy
Answer: bows. Nehemiah 4:13 records: “Therefore set I in the lowest parts of the space behind the wall, in the open places, I set there the people after their families with their swords, their spears, and their bows.”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… and said to the nobles, and to the rulers, and to the ______ of the people, Don't be you afraid of them: remember the Lord, who …” (4:14); “It happened from that time forth, that half of my ______ worked in the work, and half of them held the spears, the shields, and the …” (4:16, WEB) Hard

Answer: C. Judah. The passage specifies Judah: “Judah said, The strength of the bearers of burdens is decayed, and there is much rubbish; so that we are not able to build the wall.”

Answer: joined; half. The wording is “So we built the wall; and all the wall was joined together to half the height of it: for the people had a mind to work.” The missing terms belong in that order.

Answer: False. The actual wording is “… until we come into the midst of them, and kill them, and cause the work to cease.” It says cause, not repairing.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “and the builders, ______ had his sword girded by his side, and so built. He who sounded the trumpet was by me.” (4:18); “… held the spears from the rising of the morning until the stars ______.” (4:21, WEB) Hard

Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.

Q2. Multiple choice: What tension appears between Mordecai's confidence in deliverance and his appeal to Esther? (4:13-16) Hard
A) He promises that approaching the king carries no risk. B) He says Esther's action cannot matter. C) He expects deliverance while insisting that Esther's decision matters. D) He asks Esther to abandon her people.
Answer: C. He expects deliverance while insisting that Esther's decision matters.. The speech combines hope for the people with a costly call to act. Esther responds by requesting a fast and preparing to approach the king.

Q3. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… of the Jews, you shall not prevail against him, but shall surely ______ before him.” (6:13); “… the king's chamberlains, and hurried to bring Haman to the banquet that ______ had prepared.” (6:14, WEB) Hard
Answer: fall; Esther. The first passage reads “… of the Jews, you shall not prevail against him, but shall surely fall before him.”; the second reads “… the king's chamberlains, and hurried to bring Haman to the banquet that Esther had prepared.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q4. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… man whom the king delights to honor? Now Haman said in his ______, To whom would the king delight to do honor more than to myself?”? (6:6, WEB) Medium
A) neck B) heart C) bone D) head
Answer: B. heart. The passage specifies heart: “… man whom the king delights to honor? Now Haman said in his heart, To whom would the king delight to do honor more than to myself?”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “It was found written that ______ had told of Bigthana and Teresh, two of the king's chamberlains, of those who kept the threshold, who had sought …” (6:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Mordecai. The cited words are “It was found written that Mordecai had told of Bigthana and Teresh, two of the king's chamberlains, of those who kept the threshold, who had sought …”
Q6. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the king ordered Haman to honor ______ by leading him through the city on a horse. (6:10-11) Easy
Answer: Mordecai. Esther 6:10 records: “… said to Haman, Make haste, and take the clothing and the horse, as you have said, and do even so to Mordecai the Jew, who sits at the king's gate: let nothing fail of all that …”
Q7. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “The ______ said, "Who is in the court?" Now ______ was come into the outward court of the king's house, to speak to the king to …” (6:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: king; Haman. The wording is “The king said, "Who is in the court?" Now Haman was come into the outward court of the king's house, to speak to the king to …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q8. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “let royal clothing be brought which the king uses to wear, and the horse that the king rides on, and on the head of which a …” (6:8, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “let royal clothing be brought which the king uses to wear, and the horse that the king rides on, and on the head of which a …”
Esther 7
Q1. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “The king arose in his wrath from the banquet of ______ and went into the palace garden: and Haman stood up to make request for his …” (7:7); “Then the ______ returned out of the palace garden into the place of the banquet of wine; and Haman was fallen on the couch whereon Esther …” (7:8, WEB) Hard
A) wine; king B) oil; king C) king; wine D) wine; judges
Answer: A. wine; king. Verse 7 reads “The king arose in his wrath from the banquet of wine and went into the palace garden: and Haman stood up to make request for his …”; verse 8 reads “Then the king returned out of the palace garden into the place of the banquet of wine; and Haman was fallen on the couch whereon Esther …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “The king said again to ______ on the second day at the banquet of wine, What is your petition, queen Esther? and it shall be granted …” (7:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: Esther. The cited words are “The king said again to Esther on the second day at the banquet of wine, What is your petition, queen Esther? and it shall be granted …”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… Behold also, the gallows fifty cubits high, which Haman has made for ______, who spoke good for the king, stands in the house of Haman. The …” (7:9); “So the king and Haman came to banquet with Esther the ______.” (7:1, WEB) Hard
Answer: Mordecai; queen. The first passage reads “… Behold also, the gallows fifty cubits high, which Haman has made for Mordecai, who spoke good for the king, stands in the house of Haman. The …”; the second reads “So the king and Haman came to banquet with Esther the queen.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… to be slain, and to ______. But if we had been sold for bondservants and bondmaids, I had held my ______, although the adversary could not …” (7:4, WEB) Medium
Answer: perish; peace. The wording is “… to be slain, and to perish. But if we had been sold for bondservants and bondmaids, I had held my peace, although the adversary could not …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Esther said, An adversary and an enemy, even this wicked Elihu. Then Haman was afraid before the king and the queen.” (7:6, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Esther said, An adversary and an enemy, even this wicked Haman. Then Haman was afraid before the king and the queen.” It says Haman, not Elihu.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… Who is he, and where is he, that dared presume in his ______ to do so?”? (7:5, WEB) Medium
A) ears B) knees C) back D) heart
Answer: D. heart. The passage specifies heart: “… Who is he, and where is he, that dared presume in his heart to do so?”
Q7. True or false: Haman was hanged on the gallows he had built for Mordecai. (7:10) Easy
Answer: True. Esther 7:10 records: “So they hanged Haman on the gallows that he had prepared for Mordecai. Then was the king's wrath pacified.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… your sight, O king, and if it please the king, let my ______ be given me at my petition, and my people at my request:”? (7:3, WEB) Easy
A) curse B) thanksgiving C) deliverance D) life
Answer: D. life. Esther 7:3 uses life: “… your sight, O king, and if it please the king, let my life be given me at my petition, and my people at my request:”
Esther 8
Q1. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “Then were the king's scribes called at that time, in the six month Sivan, on the three and twentieth day of it; and it was written …” (8:9, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “Then were the king's scribes called at that time, in the third month Sivan, on the three and twentieth day of it; and it was written …” It says third, not six.
Q2. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… Jews' enemy to Esther the queen. Mordecai came before the king; for ______ had told what he was to her.” (8:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: Esther. The cited words are “… Jews' enemy to Esther the queen. Mordecai came before the king; for Esther had told what he was to her.”
Q3. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… right before the ______, and I be pleasing in his eyes, let it be written to reverse the letters devised by ______, the son of Hammedatha …” (8:5, WEB) Medium
Answer: king; Haman. The wording is “… right before the king, and I be pleasing in his eyes, let it be written to reverse the letters devised by Haman, the son of Hammedatha …” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q4. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, the king gave Mordecai his ______. (8:2) Easy
Answer: signet ring. Esther 8:2 records: “The king took off his ring, which he had taken from Haman, and gave it to Mordecai. Esther set Mordecai over the house of Haman.”
Q5. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “… it with the king's ring, and sent letters by post on horseback, ______ on swift steeds that were used in the king's service, bred of the …” (8:10); “… the provinces of king Ahasuerus, namely, on the thirteenth day of the ______ month, which is the month Adar.” (8:12, WEB) Hard
A) riding; twelfth B) twelfth; riding C) clothing; twelfth D) riding; ninety
Answer: A. riding; twelfth. Verse 10 reads “… it with the king's ring, and sent letters by post on horseback, riding on swift steeds that were used in the king's service, bred of the …”; verse 12 reads “… the provinces of king Ahasuerus, namely, on the thirteenth day of the twelfth month, which is the month Adar.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q6. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… swift steeds that were used in the king's service went out, being ______ and pressed on by the king's commandment; and the decree was given out …” (8:14); “… royal clothing of blue and white, and with a great crown of ______, and with a robe of fine linen and purple: and the city of …” (8:15, WEB) Hard
Answer: hurried; gold. The first passage reads “… swift steeds that were used in the king's service went out, being hurried and pressed on by the king's commandment; and the decree was given out …”; the second reads “… royal clothing of blue and white, and with a great crown of gold, and with a robe of fine linen and purple: and the city of …”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q7. Multiple choice: Which word completes “Then the king held out to Esther the golden ______. So Esther arose, and stood before the king.”? (8:4, WEB) Easy
A) hand B) fear C) palace D) scepter
Answer: D. scepter. Esther 8:4 uses scepter: “Then the king held out to Esther the golden scepter. So Esther arose, and stood before the king.”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “Then the king Ahasuerus said to Esther the queen and to ______ the Jew, See, I have given Esther the house of Haman, and him they …”? (8:7, WEB) Medium

Esther 9
Q1. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “… as sought their hurt: and no man could withstand them; for the ______ of them was fallen on all the peoples.” (9:2, WEB) Easy
Answer: fear. The cited words are “… as sought their hurt: and no man could withstand them; for the fear of them was fallen on all the peoples.”
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “Therefore do the Jews of the villages, who ______ in the unwalled towns, make the fourteenth day of the month Adar a day of gladness and …” (9:19); “because Haman the son of Hammedatha, the ______, the enemy of all the Jews, had plotted against the Jews to destroy them, and had cast Pur, …” (9:24, WEB) Hard
A) seed; Agagite B) Agagite; dwell C) dwell; mourning D) dwell; Agagite
Answer: D. dwell; Agagite. Verse 19 reads “Therefore do the Jews of the villages, who dwell in the unwalled towns, make the fourteenth day of the month Adar a day of gladness and …”; verse 24 reads “because Haman the son of Hammedatha, the Agagite, the enemy of all the Jews, had plotted against the Jews to destroy them, and had cast Pur, …”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… together on the fourteenth day also of the month Adar, and killed three hundred men in Shushan; but they didn't lay their hand on the spoil.” (9:15, WEB) Medium
Answer: True. The actual wording is “… together on the fourteenth day also of the month Adar, and killed three hundred men in Shushan; but they didn't lay their hand on the spoil.”
Q4. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “the ______ sons of ______ the son of Hammedatha, the Jew's enemy, killed they; but they didn't lay their hand on the spoil.” (9:10, WEB) Medium
Answer: ten; Haman. The wording is “the ten sons of Haman the son of Hammedatha, the Jew's enemy, killed they; but they didn't lay their hand on the spoil.” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q5. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “but when the matter came before the ______, he commanded by letters that his wicked device, which he had devised against the Jews, should return on …” (9:25); “Then Esther the queen, the daughter of Abihail, and ______ the Jew, wrote with all authority to confirm this second letter of Purim.” (9:29, WEB) Hard
Answer: king; Mordecai. The first passage reads “but when the matter came before the king, he commanded by letters that his wicked device, which he had devised against the Jews, should return on …”; the second reads “Then Esther the queen, the daughter of Abihail, and Mordecai the Jew, wrote with all authority to confirm this second letter of Purim.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
Q6. Multiple choice: Which word completes “In Shushan the palace the Jews killed and destroyed ______ hundred men.”? (9:6, WEB) Easy
A) five B) seventeen C) twelve D) seven
Answer: A. five. Esther 9:6 uses five: “In Shushan the palace the Jews killed and destroyed five hundred men.”
Q7. True or false: The Jews celebrated the feast of Purim after their deliverance. (9:20-22) Easy
Answer: True. Esther 9:20 records: “Mordecai wrote these things, and sent letters to all the Jews who were in all the provinces of the king Ahasuerus, both near and far,”
Q8. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “The king said to ______ the queen, The Jews have slain and destroyed five hundred men in Shushan the palace, and the ten sons of Haman; …”? (9:12, WEB) Medium
A) Aaron B) Asher C) Esther D) Joseph
Answer: C. Esther. The passage specifies Esther: “The king said to Esther the queen, The Jews have slain and destroyed five hundred men in Shushan the palace, and the ten sons of Haman; …”
Esther 10
Q1. Fill in the blank: Supply the missing words in order: “… greatness of ______, whereunto the king advanced him, aren't they written in the book of the chronicles of the kings of Media and ______?” (10:2, WEB) Medium
Answer: Mordecai; Persia. The wording is “… greatness of Mordecai, whereunto the king advanced him, aren't they written in the book of the chronicles of the kings of Media and Persia?” The missing terms belong in that order.
Q2. Multiple choice: Which pair completes BOTH passages in order? “For Mordecai the Jew was next to ______ Ahasuerus, and great among the Jews, and accepted of the multitude of his brothers, seeking the good of …” (10:3); “The king ______ laid a tribute on the land, and on the isles of the sea.” (10:1, WEB) Hard
A) elders; Ahasuerus B) Ahasuerus; king C) king; brothers D) king; Ahasuerus
Answer: D. king; Ahasuerus. Verse 3 reads “For Mordecai the Jew was next to king Ahasuerus, and great among the Jews, and accepted of the multitude of his brothers, seeking the good of …”; verse 1 reads “The king Ahasuerus laid a tribute on the land, and on the isles of the sea.”. These establish the first and second answers respectively.
Q3. Multiple choice: Which detail belongs in this passage: “… of Mordecai, whereunto the king advanced him, aren't they written in the ______ of the chronicles of the kings of Media and Persia?”? (10:2, WEB) Medium
A) gate B) lamp C) book D) lampstand
Answer: C. book. The passage specifies book: “… of Mordecai, whereunto the king advanced him, aren't they written in the book of the chronicles of the kings of Media and Persia?”
Q4. Multiple choice: Which word completes “… him, aren't they written in the book of the chronicles of the ______ of Media and Persia?”? (10:2, WEB) Easy
A) kings B) daughters C) father D) son
Answer: A. kings. Esther 10:2 uses kings: “… him, aren't they written in the book of the chronicles of the kings of Media and Persia?”
Q5. Fill in the blank: Complete the quotation: “The king Ahasuerus laid a tribute on the ______, and on the isles of the sea.” (10:1, WEB) Easy
Answer: land. The cited words are “The king Ahasuerus laid a tribute on the land, and on the isles of the sea.”
Q6. Fill in the blank: In this paraphrase, mordecai was second in rank to King ______. (10:3) Easy
Answer: Ahasuerus. Esther 10:3 records: “For Mordecai the Jew was next to king Ahasuerus, and great among the Jews, and accepted of the multitude of his brothers, seeking the good of his people, and speaking peace to all his seed.”
Q7. True or false: The following quotation matches the cited verse: “… multitude of his brothers, seeking the good of his people, and speaking righteousness to all his seed.” (10:3, WEB) Medium
Answer: False. The actual wording is “… multitude of his brothers, seeking the good of his people, and speaking peace to all his seed.” It says peace, not righteousness.
Q8. Fill in the blank: Connect the two passages by supplying both missing terms: “… Mordecai the Jew was next to king Ahasuerus, and great among the ______, and accepted of the multitude of his brothers, seeking the good of his …” (10:3); “The king Ahasuerus laid a tribute on the land, and on the ______ of the sea.” (10:1, WEB) Hard
Answer: Jews; isles. The first passage reads “… Mordecai the Jew was next to king Ahasuerus, and great among the Jews, and accepted of the multitude of his brothers, seeking the good of his …”; the second reads “The king Ahasuerus laid a tribute on the land, and on the isles of the sea.”. Keeping the details with their own passages gives the stated order.
